# THE SHORELINE BENCH, plan step 4 slice 0 (ruling B8 item 1; survey §6; design §19 WHAT FOLLOWS)
# written by `cargo run --release -p vd-bins --example shoreline_bench`; 10 threads for the column passes (COST on one); one job on the machine
WORLD generator 21 (vd_terrain::GENERATOR_VERSION)
THE NAMED CHOICES (ruling T9: each with its default and its source):
  - k = 0.25 (W6's quarter; the owner's word owed, design §15 Q3)
  - K = 4 (D5, 1/k; the owner's word owed, design §15 Q4)
  - g_min = 1 m per node (D4: the rows' own unit)
  - the fade = the recipe's quintic over ONE node of the first-order distance (D4: the read's own reach)
  - the mirror = all sixteen words (finding 1 fix (a); the corners only is the bench's second slice)
  - the held read = the mean of both pass orders (finding 11)
  - the governing candidate = the lowest-level wet one; if dry, the least d; a tie goes to the higher level (W15.1)
  - the candidate cap = 4, the nearest wet node first (finding 23; an overflow is counted, never dropped silently)
  - F = R1 - 3 (finding 7; D6): the rungs from F up take the wet majority of their rung-F point sides, a tie wet (W15.1 item 4), the water with the most samples (a tie the higher level)
  - the magnitude at a folded rung = the rung's own |G| of the fold's water at the column's centre, signed by the fold's side (this bench, stated)
  - O1's clamp: telescoped from R1, c_s = kappa x one cell of rung s+1 in nodes; kappa = 1 (O1), 0.25 (O1q, design §5.6) (survey §6.1, finding 3)
  - the terrace inside the band = faded with beta (one field at the line)
  - A_r = body.relief_bound(r) (D5: a charter fact, an over-bound)
  - a dry column's standing water = today's land rule (D7): the sea where its read stencil holds a sea node, else none; a pocket's floor none
  - the sheet's level over a column L_c = the highest l over its 3 x 3 columns (water_sheet_sided: a vertex's level is the highest of its four columns)
  - transects: 256 per water kind (the sea; the lakes pooled) per stand — the plan's 128 give about 128 matched pairs, under the 200 its band is judged on — spacing >= 1000 m along C0's B = W contour at rung 7 in W2; half-length 1 node (sea), 0.5 node (lake); sample step cell(r)/4; the whole transect from rung 5 up, windows of +-8 coarser cells round the coarser rung's crossings under it
  - the SHORE-STEP match: within 4 coarser cells; beyond that it is a fold event (finding 8)
  - WALL: Q_r = the p99.9 of the raw ground's neighbour steps in the window; band 0.001 of the shore pairs; judged on >= 1000 pairs (finding 19)
  - CHECK-R0-F64: the f64 shore against the integer one, max |d| <= 0.001 m
  - the panels' budget: 12582912 bytes (picture choices, not laws)
  - N2's side hold = 1 (1: a side-dry column that carries a standing water stands over it by k x |its governing field|; the side decides, the sheet never covers it)
  - N2's fold names its water by = the one rule (1 the one rule: a lake when the lake samples are at least half of the wet ones, at the highest lake level among them — W15.1's lake share, the rule a count pyramid carries to the top rung; 0 the record: the water with the most samples, a tie the higher level; the build design's S0 item 4, Q7)
  - N2's default rule = 1 (1: a slot for a water with no wet node in the column's block reads every word W + band, every distance 3.5 nodes, no presence — the build design §1.2 item 9, so a host with the shore band and a host with the rows read one word; it fires only where the fold names such a water, from rung 14 up; 0: the rows' own heights)
  - N2's wet tie = 1 (1: a side-wet column stands at least one length unit under its water, so the sheet draws it wet; 0: a column whose side field reads exactly nought stands AT its level and the sheet draws it dry — from rung 14 up a column's centre is its node cell's middle, where a straight coast's clamped words read nought; S0 item 5)
  - N2's lone hold = 1 (1: a column with no water in its block whose side is dry stands over its standing water by k x band, the default rule's slot; 0: the land rule alone, under which a far column's ground can stand under the sea it reads; S0 item 5)
  - N2's fold kernel = 0 (under the one naming rule: 0 the column's own footprint; 1 the tent, the column and its eight neighbours at its rung weighted 1-2-1 per axis; 2 the tent only where the column's own samples disagree; 3 the own footprint with no lone wet column; ruling B15 item 1, the far lake flicker)
  - the skin's window = a cell over 2^1 at its finest, a margin of 22 finest texels, 5 levels (the far-water design D: 1, 22 and 5); the filter's level rule 1 (0 the level of P_max/N, N = ceil(P_max/P_min) probes, the GL extension's sketch; 1 the level of P_max/eta, eta the ratio itself, the Vulkan specification's sketch)
  - N2's pocket variant = 2 (0 the record; 1 a POCKET-word column exempt from the sea's side hold; 2 = 1 and the standing water's floor only where the sea governs; 3 = 1 and that floor relaxed by the sea's presence; the build design's S0 item 3, Q11)
  - N2's reach per octave = the law (1: the law C_kind x wavelength, C_kind measured over unit octaves of the recipe's own noise; 0: 15/8 x RMS height / RMS slope measured over the table's own octaves)
  - N2's word band = 30 m (each mirrored word held within it of the level; inf = N1's words; ruling B10 item 1, the steep coasts' notches; 30 m a stated choice of the second round, the owner's word owed)
  - N2's words as a signed distance = 0 (1: word = level + band x (the distance in nodes to the nearest block node of the other side - 1/2), signed by the side; 0: the band's clamp)
  - N2's side as a smoothed mask = 0 (1: S = band x sum w.side / sum w over the block, side +1 dry -1 wet, w = (1 - r^2/4)^3 within 2 nodes of the column; the slope by central differences of 1/64 node)
  - N2's form = 2 (3 a fade per fine octave in HEIGHT: octave k scaled by the recipe's quintic of |S| over 3 x the column's factor x its RMS height at share one; 2 a ramp per fine octave: each octave's term scaled by the recipe's quintic of the first-order distance over its own reach 15/8 x its RMS height / its RMS slope, both measured at share one over the sphere; 1 one ramp for the whole fine half over 15/8 x lift / slope, lift = the column's factor x the fine half's mean at share one at the rung, slope = the bank below; the wet side N1's own in both; 0 the bent residual below, a refuted control)
  - N2's first form: the conditioned residual rho at the rung, kept whole to 0.5 of its room and bent (tanh) toward the room past it; the room on the side it can cross = the node-scale surface's height over W6's hold, on the other side = the bank (the solve's TAN_REPOSE 0.7 (here: the fine half's measured RMS slope at the rung times the factor), times the first-order distance to the line); faded in by the presence
  overrides this run: n2_fold_rule=1, skin_sub=1, skin_margin=22, skin_lod=1
THE RULES: R0, C0, N1, N1-nofold, O1, O1-nofold, O1q, N2
